1b1(sher phyin): @#//'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa khri brgyad stong pa/bam po nyi shu gsum pa/de nas bcom ldan 'das la tshe dang ldan 
1b2(sher phyin): pa kun dga' bos 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ci ltar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i ming yongs su brjod pa mdzad pa ltar/sbyin pa'i pha 
1b3(sher phyin): rol tu phyin pa'i bsngags pa yongs su brjod pa ma mdzad lags so//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dang /brtson 'grus 
1b4(sher phyin): kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i bsngags payongs su brjod pa ma mdzed lags so//de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres 
1b5(sher phyin): pa bco brgyad kyi bar gyi ming yongs su brjod pam mdzad lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo la 'di 
1b6(sher phyin): skad ces bka' stsal to//kun dga' bo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa ni 'di lta ste pha rol tu phyin pa lnga po rnams dang /de bzhin du sbyar te/sangs rgyas kyi chos 
1b7(sher phyin): ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar gyi sngon du 'gro ba yin no//kun dga' bo 'di snyem du sems/sbyin pa rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su ma sngos par pha rol tu 
1b8(sher phyin): phyin pa'i ming 'thob par 'gyur snyam 'am/kun dga' bo 'di ji snyam du sems/tshul khrims dang bzod pa dang brtson 'grus dang bsam gtan dang shes rab rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa 
2a1(sher phyin): @#//nyid du yongs su ma bsngos pa pha rol tu phyin pa'i ming 'thob par 'gyur snyam 'am/kun dga' bos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das de ni ma lags na/bcom ldan 'das sbyin pa rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su bsngos pa dang /tshul khrims dang /bzod 
2a2(sher phyin): pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bsam gtan dang /shes rab rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid du yongs su bsngos bas/ji ltar pha rol tu phyin pa'i ming thob par 'gyur ba lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo sbyin pa dang /tshul khrims dang/
2a3(sher phyin): bzod pa dang /brtson 'grus dang/bsam gtan dang /shesarab gnyis su med pa'i tshul gyis rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid du yongs su bsngos na/pha rol tu phyin pa'i ming thos par 'gyur ro//len pa med pa'i tshul dang /mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rnam pa tham
2a4(sher phyin): sa cad mkhyen pa nyid du yangs su bsngos na/pha rol du phyin pa'i ming thob par gyur ro//kun dga' bos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das sbyin ba gnyis su ma mchis pa'i tshul gyis rnam pa thabs cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su bsngos pas ji ltar pha rol tu phyin pa'i
2a5(sher phyin): ming thob par 'gyur ba la gas/bcom ldan 'das tshul khrims dang/bzod pa dang /brtson 'gyus dang/bsam gtan dang/shes rab gnyis su ma mchis pa'i tshil gyis rnam pa thams cadamakhyen pa nyid du yongs subasngos bas/ji ltar pha rol tu phyin pa'
2a6(sher phyin): ming thong par 'gyur ba lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo gzugs gnyis su med pa dang /tshor ba dang 'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang/rnam par shes pa dang/byang chub kyi bar du gnyis su med pa'i tshul gyis yongs su bsngos pa'i
2a7(sher phyin): phyir ro/kun dga' bos gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gzugs gnyis su ma mchis pa'i tshal ji lta bu lags/tshor ba dang/'du shes dang 'du byed rnams dang/rnam bar shes pa gnyis sum mchis pa'i tshul ji lta bu lags/byang chub kyi bar du gnyi sum mchis
2a8(sher phyin): pa'i tshul ji lta bu lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka stsal pa/'di ltar gzugs ni gzugs kyis stong ngo//tshor ba ni tshor bas stong ngo/'du shes ni 'du shes kyis stong ngo/'du byed rnams ni 'du byed rnams kyis stong ngo/rnam par shes pa ni rnam par shes pas
2b1(sher phyin): stong ngo/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar gzugs dang /tshor ba dang 'du shes dang /'du byed rnams dang /rnam par shes pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa de ni gnyis su med cing gnyis su dbyer med pa dang /pha rol tu phyinapa dang /byang chub kyi bar de ni gnyis su med cing
2b2(sher phyin): gnyis su dbyer med pa'i phyir ro//kun dga' bo de bas na 'di la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nyid pha rol tu phyin pa lnga po rnams dang/byang chub dang/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar gyi sngon du 'gro ba yin no//kun dga' bo 'di lta ste dper
2b3(sher phyin): na/sa chen po la brten na tshogs pa'i dbang gis sa bon rnams skye bar 'gyur ro//kun dga' bo debazhin du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa la rten na/pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad dang /byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum bcu rtsa bdun dang/rnam pa thams
2b4(sher phyin): cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du skye bar 'gyur ro//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen panyid la brten na yang/pha rol tu phyin pa tham sa cad dang/byang chub kyi phyogs kyi cho sa sum bcu rtsa bdun dang /stobs bcu dang/mi 'jigs pa rnams dang/so so yang
2b5(sher phyin): dag par rig pa rnams dang/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams skye bar 'gyur ro/kun dga' bo de bas na/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nyid pha rol tu phyin pa 'di dag dang/sangs rgyas kyi chos la 'dres pa bcwa brgyad dang/rnam pa thams
2b6(sher phyin): cad la mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar gyi yongs su 'dren pa yin no//brgya byin gyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di 'dzin pa dang/'chad ba dang klog pa dang kun chub par bgyid pa dang /
2b7(sher phyin): tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid pa de/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i yon tan gang dag yongs su 'dzin pa'i yon tan de dag thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis yongs su brjod pa ma mdzad lagaso//
2b8(sher phyin): bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phin pa bzung zhing tshul bzhin du yid la bgyis pas ni/dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam rnams 'jig rten du 'byung lags so//bsam gtan bzhi dang/tshad med pa bzhi dang/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi dang
3a1(sher phyin): @#//
3b1(sher phyin): gyi phung po dang/shes rab kyi phung po dang/rnam par grol ba'i phung po dang/rnam par gro ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i phung po tshad med pa dang ldan par 'gyur la/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems dang yang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ro//kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'am rigs
3b2(sher phyin): kyi bu mo tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa'i bar du byed pa de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dang 'dra bar rig par bya ste/rnam pa thams cad mkhyeen pa nyid kyi sems dang yang 'bral par mi 'gyur ro//kau shika nyan thos dang/rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi tshul khrims kyi phung
3b3(sher phyin): po dang/ting nge 'dzin gyi phung po dang/shes rab kya phung po dang/rnam par 'grol ba'i phung po dang/rna ma par grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i phug po gang yin pa'i tshul khrims dang/ting nge 'dzin dang/shes rab dang/rnam par grol ba dang/rnam par grol ba' ye shes mthong
3b4(sher phyin): ba'i phung po de ni/kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'ang rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa rnams kyi tshul khrims kyi phung po nas/rnam par grol ba ye shes mthong ba'i phung po'i bar gang yin pa'i tshul khrimasakyi phung po nas/rnam par 'grol ba'i ye shes
3b5(sher phyin): mthong ba'i phung po'i bar de'i rgya'i char yang nye bar mi 'gro zhing/stong gi cha dang/'bum gyi cha dang/grangs dang cha dang/bgrang ba dang dpe dang/rgyur yang mi bzod do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar rigs kyi bu dang rigs kyi bu mo de dag gi sems ni nyan thos dang/rang sangs
3b6(sher phyin): rgyas kyi sa las rnam par grol zhing/chos gang dang yang bral ba med la/skyes pa yang med pa'i phyir ro//kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kya pha rol tu phyin pa 'di yi ger bris nas 'chang zhing me tog dang/bdug pa dang spos dang me tog phreng ba dang/
3b7(sher phyin): byug pa dang phye ma dang/gos dang gdugs dang/rgyal mtshan dang/ba da na dag gis bkur sti byed/bla mar byed ri mor byed/mchod par byed pa gang yin pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs gyi bu mo de dag gis kyang tshe 'di dang/tshe phyi ma'i yon tan rnams thob par re bar bya'o//
3b8(sher phyin): de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang/bcom ldan 'das la lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kya bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzin pa dang/'chang pa dang klog pa dang/kun chub par bgyid pa dang/
4a1(sher phyin): @#//tshul bzhin du yid la bgyid cing /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di la me tog dang /bdug pa dang spos dang /me tog dang phreng ba dang /byug pa dang phye ma dang /gos dang gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan dag gis bkur stir bgyid/bla mar bgyid 
4a2(sher phyin): ri mor bgyid/mchod par bgyid pa gang lags pa de la bdag gis kyang rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par bsrung ba dang bskyab pa dang sba bar bgyi'o//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems dang yang 'bral bar mi 'gyur lags so//de skad ces gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 
4a3(sher phyin): 'das kyis lha'i dbang po brgya byin la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di kha ton du byed pa de'i gan du lha'i bu 'bum phrag mang po nyan pa'i phyir 'ong bar 'gyur te/lha'i bu rnams rigs kyi bu 'am rigs 
4a4(sher phyin): kyi bu mo de dag la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa dang ldan pa'i spobs pa nye bar bsgrub par sems par 'gyur ro//bdag gi tshe chos smra ba de dag smra bar mi 'dod pa na'ang lha'i bu de dag nyid chos la gus pas spobs pa nye bar sgrub par sems par 'gyur te/kau shi ka rigs kyi 
4a5(sher phyin): bu'am rigs kyi bu mo gang zhig shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyis pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang /klog pa dang /kun chub par byed pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed cing de glegs bam du yang bris nas/me tog dang bdug pa dang spos dang /me tog phreng dang /byug pa dang phye ma dang gos dang 
4a6(sher phyin): gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang ba dan dag gis sti stang byed/bla mar byed ri mor byed mchod par byed pa de ni tshe 'di'i yon tan de dag kyang thob par 'gyur ro//kau shi ka gzhan yang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di ston pa de dag ni 'khor bzhi po dag 
4a7(sher phyin): gi mdun du bdag la la zhig dkar gcog cing glan ka tshol du 'ong ngo snyam du sems zhum par mi 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo de dag la shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa 'di srung zhing skyob la sbed par byed pa'i phyir ro//'di ltar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na phal ni 
4a8(sher phyin): 'jig rten pa rnams dang /'jig rten las 'das pa rnams dang /zag pa dang bcas pa rnams dang /zag par med pa rnams dang /thun mong ba rnams dang /thun mong ma yin pa rnams dang /dge ba rnams dang /mi dge ba rnams dang /'dus byas rnams dang /'dus ma byas rnams dang /nyan thos kyi chos rnams dang /rang sangs 
4b1(sher phyin): rgyas kyi chos rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa'i chos rnams dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams dang /chos thams cad tha dad pa med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo nang stong pa nyid la gnas pa dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi bar la gnas 
4b2(sher phyin): pa des ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la klan ka tshol ba yang dag par rjes su mi mthong zhing /glan ka tshol bar byed pa de yang yang dag par rjes su mi 'thong ba'i phyir ro//de ltar na rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyis bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapas yongs su zin pa de la ni glan ka tshol ba 
4b3(sher phyin): med do//kau shi ka gzhan yang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs gyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa'i bar du byed pa ni/sems zhum par mi 'gyur/kun tu zhum par mi 'gyur/skrag par mi 'gyur/dngang bar mi 'gyur/kun tu dngang 
4b4(sher phyin): bar mi 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na//'di ltar rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo de dag gis ni gang zhum pa 'am/kun tu zhum pa'am/skrag pa 'am dngang ba 'am/kun tu dngang bar 'gyur ba'i dngos po yang dag par rjes su ma mthong ba'i phyir ro//kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'am 
4b5(sher phyin): rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha'i rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzin pa dang /'chang ba dang klog pa dang /kun chub par byed pa dang /tshul bzhin du yod la byed cing /me tog dang bdug pa dang /spos dang me tog phreng dang /byug pa dang phye ma dang /gos dang gdugs dang rgyal mtshan 
4b6(sher phyin): dang /ba dan dag gis sti stang byed/bla mar byed ri mor byed/mchod pa byed pa de ni tshe 'di'i yon tan de dag yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur ro//kau shi ka gzhan yang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang la la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzinapa dang /'chang ba 
4b7(sher phyin): dang /klog pa dang /kun chub bar byed pa dang /tshul bzhan du yid la byed cing de glegs bam du yang bris te/me tog dang bdug pa dag/spos dang me tog phreng dag/byug pa dang phye ma dag/gos dang gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang ba dan dag gis sti stangs su byed/bla mar byed 
4b8(sher phyin): ri mor byed/mchod pa bye ba de ni/pha ma dang mdza' bo dang /phyi 'khor dang gnyen dang /snag gi gnyen mtshams rnams dang /dge sbyong dang bram ze rnams kyi snying du sdug par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' de ni phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad na bzhugs 
5a1(sher phyin): @#//pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang /rang sangs rgyas rnams dang /dgra bcom pa rnams dang /slob pa thams cad dang /mi slob pa de dag thams cad dang /mi slobapa de dag thams cad la sdug par 'gyur ro//'jig 
5a2(sher phyin): rten lha dang bcas pa/bdud dang bcas pa/tshangs pa dang bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bram zer bcas pa'i skye dgu rnams dang /lha dang mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa rnams kyi yid du 'ong bar 'gyur ro//spobs pa mi chod pa dang yang ldan par 'gyur ro//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu 
5a3(sher phyin): phyin pa mi chod pa dang yang lda na par 'gyur ro//tshul khrims dang /bzod pa dang brtson 'grus dang /bsam gtan dang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa mi chod pa dang yangaldan par 'gyur ro//nang stong pa nyid dang /dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi bar du mi chod pa dang /dran 
5a4(sher phyin): pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams dang /yang dag pa'i spong ba rnams dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams dang /dbang po rnams dang /stobs rnams dang /byang chub kyi yan lag rnams dang /lam rnams dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang /mi 'jigs pa bzhi mi chod pa dang /so so 
5a5(sher phyin): dag par rig pa bzhi mi chod pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bcwa brgyad mi chod pa dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo dang gzungs kyi sgo rnams mi chod pa dang /mngon par shes pa rnams dang /sems can yongs su smin par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bya 
5a6(sher phyin): ba mi chod pa dang /rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid mi chod pa dang ldan par 'gyur ro//mu stegs can gyi rgol ba byung ngo cog la/chos dang ldan pas tshar gcod nus par 'gyur ro//kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzin pa 
5a7(sher phyin): dang /'chang ba dang klog pa dang /kun chub par byed pa dang /tshul bzhin du yid la byed cing de yi ger yang bris te me tog dang bdug pa dang /spos dang me tog phreng dang /byug pa dang phye ma dang /gos dang gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang ba dan dag gis sti stang du byed/bla mar byed 
5a8(sher phyin): ri mor byed mchod par byed pa ni tshe 'dri dang tshe phyi ma'i yon tan de dag kyang yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid kyi sems dang yang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ro//kau shi ka gzhan yang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyis bu mo shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi na pa 'di 
5b1(sher phyin): glegs bam du bris te/'chang bar byed pa der/kau shi ka stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na 'khod pa'i rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha'i bu bla na med pa'i byang chub la yang dag par zhugs pa gang yin pa de dag der lhags nas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin 
5b2(sher phyin): pa 'di 'dzin par byed/'chad par byed/klog par byed/kun chub par byed cing /phyag byas te btud nas phyir 'dod par 'gyur ro//de bzhin du sum bcu rtsa gsum pa rnams dang /'thab bral rnams dang /dga' ldan rnams dang /'phrul dga' rnams dang /gzhan 'phrul 
5b3(sher phyin): dbang byed rnams dang /tshangs ris rnams dang /tshangs pa'i mdun na 'don rnams dang /tshangs 'khor rnams dang /'od chung rnams dang /tshad med 'od rnams dang /'od gsal rnams dang /dge chung rnams dang /tshad me dge rnams dang /dge rgyas rnams dang /'bras 
5b4(sher phyin): bu che ba rnams dang /tshangs pa gang dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu smon lam gtab pa de dag kyang der lhags nas/shes rab? kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di gzung ba dang /bcang ba dang /klag pa dang kun chub par byas nas phyir 'dod par 'gyur? ro//
5b5(sher phyin): kau shi ka de la gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu mi che ba dang /shin tu mthong ba dang /gya nom snang dang /mi gdung ba dang /'og min gyi lha'i bu gang yin pa de dag kyang der lhag sa nas/shes rab gyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di bzung ba dang /bcang ba ba dang klag pa dang /kun chub par byas 
5b6(sher phyin): te/phyag byas nas phyir 'dod par sems par 'gyur ro//kau shi ka rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo des kyang 'di snyam du sems skyed par bya ste/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams dag na 'khod pa'i lha'i bu rgyal chen bzhi'i ris su gtogs pa nas 'bras bu che ba 
5b7(sher phyin): man chad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la yang dag par zhugs pa de gang yin pa de dag dang /gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lta'i bu gang yin pa rnams dang /gzhan yang lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang mi 'am 
5b8(sher phyin): ca dang lto 'phye chen po gang yin pa de dag 'dir lhags nas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di gzung ba dang /bcang ba dang /klag pa dang kun chub par byas shing phyag byas te/mchod pa byas pa rnams phyir deng shig/chos kyi sbyin pa 'di yang de dag la byin par gyur cig/
6a1(sher phyin): @#//snyam du sems skyed par bya'o//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams dag na 'khod pa'i rgyal chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha'i bu gang yin pa rnams dang /'bras bu che ba'i lha'i bu man chad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la yang dag par zhugs 
6a2(sher phyin): pa gang yin pa rnams dang /gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu gang yinapa de dag kyang 'dir lhags nas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di 'dzin par byed/'chang bar byed/klog par byed/kun chub par byed cing phyag byas te/btud nas phyir 'dod par sems 
6a3(sher phyin): so//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na 'khod pa'i rgyal chen bzhI'i ris kyi lha'i bu gang yin+pa pa rnams dang /'og min gyi lha'i bu man chad dang /phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyis khams dag na 'khod pa'i rgyu la chen bzhi'i ris kyi lha'i ba rnams 
6a4(sher phyin): dang /'og min gyi lha man chad gang yin pa de dag kyang rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo de la bsrung ba dang skyob pa dang /sbed par byed cing /de la glags tshol ba gang gis kyang glags mi rnyed de/las kyi rnam par smin pa ni ma gtogs so//kau shi ka rigs 
6a5(sher phyin): kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo de dag ni tshe 'di'i yon tan de dag kyang yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyis bu mo de dag ni bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la yang dag par zhugs pa/sems can thams 
6a6(sher phyin): cad kyi skyabs bya ba dang /sems can thams cad yongs su mi gtong ba dang /sems can tham cad la phan pa dang /bde bar bya ba'i ched du yang dag par zhugs pa'i phyir ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom

18a1(sher phyin): @#//pa bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa'i don bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na/rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo gang de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gdung de dag la/bdag nyid kyang me tog la sogs pas/sti stang du bgyid/bla mar bgyid/ri mo bgyid/
18a2(sher phyin): mchod pa bgyid pa bas/gzhan dag la yang stsol bar bgyid pa de ni/de las bsod nams ches mang du skyed lags so//de bzhin gshegs pas don gyi dbang de gzigs nas sems can gyi tshogs kyi nang na/sems can de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku 
18a3(sher phyin): gdung gis 'dul bar 'gyur ba rnams la thugs rje chen po bskyed de/de bzhin gshegs pas rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/rdo rje lta bu'i sku bshig ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gadrung rnams su byin gyis brlabs lags so//bcom 
18a4(sher phyin): ldan 'das tha na de bzhin gshegs pa yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bdud yungs 'bru tsam la mchod pa bgyid pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba ni yongs su mya ngan las 'da' par gyur gyi bar du de dag thams cad kyis de'i mtha' rtogs par 
18a5(sher phyin): rdo mi thog lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/kau shi ka de de bzhin no//de de bzhin te/kau shi ka rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo gang la la zhig shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di la me tog dang /bdug pa dang /sbos dang /me tog phreng dang /
18a6(sher phyin): byug pa dang /phye ma dang /gos dang /gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /ba da na dag gis sti stang du byed pa dang /bla mar byed pa dang /ri mor byed pa dang /mchod pa byed pa bas gang gis yi ger bris nas gzhan dag la sbyin par byed na/rigs kyi bu'am rigs 
18a7(sher phyin): kyi bu mo de de las ches bsod nams mang du bskyed do//kau shi ka gzhan yang rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi pu mo gang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi na pa 'di ji ltar lung phog pa bzhin du gzhan dag gi drung du song ste/'chad cing ston pa dang /rjod cing 'grel pa dang /
18a8(sher phyin): gsal bar byed na/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo de ni/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs gyi bu mo snga ma bas/ches bsod nams mang du skyed de/ston pa 'am bla ma'i gnas tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa mkhas pa gang yang rung ba zhig yin par shes par bya'o//
18b1(sher phyin): de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di la ston pa yang gzhan la/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yang gzhan pa ni ma yin gyi/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa 'di nyid ston pa yin la/ston pa nyid shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi pa yin pas na/'di ltar shes rab kyi pha 
18b2(sher phyin): rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid ston pa y-in par blta'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa 'di la bslabs 
18b3(sher phyin): pas byung ba yin la/tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa mkhas pa gang yang rung ba dang /phyir mi ldog pa la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa de dag kyang shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa 'di nyid la bslabs pas/bla na med pa yang dag par 
18b4(sher phyin): rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i phyir ro//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin/pa 'di la bslabs pas/nyan thos kyi theg pa pa rnams kyis kyang dgra bcom pa nyid thob par gyur to//rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa pa rnams kyi kyang 
18b5(sher phyin): rang byang chub thob par gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyang byang chub sems dpa'i skyon med pa la zhugs so//'jug go/'jug par 'gyur ro//kau shi ka de bas na rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
18b6(sher phyin): rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas rnams mngon sum du bya bar 'dod pa dang /me tog dang /bdug pa dang /spos dang /me tog phreng dang /byug pa dang /phye ma dang /gos dang /gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan dag gis sti stang du bya ba dang /bla mar bya ba dang /ri mor bya ba 
18b7(sher phyin): dang mchod pa bya bar 'dod pa des shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di nyid glegs bam la bris te/me tog dang /bdug ba dang /spos dang /me tag phreng dang /byug pa pa dang /phye ma dang gos dang gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan pa dang /ba dan dag gi sti stang du bya/bla mar bya 
18b8(sher phyin): ri mor bya mchod pa bya'o//da bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/don gyi dbang 'di mthong nas/'di snyam du bsams te/ngas gang la brten te gnas par bya/gang zhig la ni sti stang bya/bla mar bya/ri mor bya/
